   570	    assert "POWER IS NOT AN EXCLUSION" in pw["scope_warning"]
   571	
   572	
   573	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   574	# 7. C6 — Holm on raw p; the family is derived, not hand-listed
   575	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   576	def test_holm_step_down_is_correct():
   577	    adj = holm([0.01, 0.02, 0.03])
   578	    assert adj[0] == pytest.approx(0.03)      # 3 * 0.01
   579	    assert adj == sorted(adj)                 # step-down enforces monotonicity
   580	    assert all(0.0 <= a <= 1.0 for a in adj)
   581	
   582	
   583	def test_holm_on_raw_p_can_differ_from_holm_on_rounded_p():
   584	    """v1 fed 4-decimal rounded p into Holm. At the 5% boundary that flips a
   585	    verdict, so the raw values must survive all the way to serialization."""
   586	    raw = [0.0166667, 0.4, 0.9]
   587	    rounded = [round(p, 4) for p in raw]
   588	    assert holm(raw)[0] != holm(rounded)[0]
   589	
   590	
   591	def test_union_and_intersection_claims_get_opposite_multiplicity_treatment():
   592	    """The two families need OPPOSITE corrections, and the reason is logical, not
   593	    stylistic. Pinned because "be consistent, Holm everything" is the tempting
   594	    wrong move.
   595	
   596	    - "flow helps SOMEWHERE" is a UNION of alternatives: ten shots at finding an
   597	      effect, so the family-wise error rate must be controlled -> Holm.
   598	    - "flow helps NOWHERE by >= m" is an INTERSECTION: it may be asserted only if
   599	      EVERY cell rejects, which is an intersection-union test (Berger 1982) and
   600	      holds at level alpha with each cell UNADJUSTED. Holm there would inflate
   601	      type-II error and buy no type-I protection.
   602	
   603	    Concretely: a set of p-values that a conjunction claim should accept must be
   604	    accepted unadjusted, while the same p-values under a union claim must not all
   605	    survive Holm.
   606	    """
   607	    p = [0.01, 0.02, 0.03, 0.04]
   608	
   609	    # conjunction (exclusion): every cell rejects at an unadjusted 5%
   610	    assert all(x < 0.05 for x in p)
   611	
   612	    # union (GW): Holm must NOT wave all four through
   613	    assert not all(x < 0.05 for x in holm(p))
   614	
   615	
   616	def test_registry_captures_every_test_including_the_smearing_runs(panel):
   617	    """v1 hand-listed "EVERY DM test" and missed the 8 smearing ones. The family
   618	    must be DERIVED, so a new robustness run cannot be forgotten."""
   619	    evaluate_cell(panel, "HAR+ctrl", "H1_absflow", family="primary")
   620	    evaluate_cell(panel, "HAR+ctrl", "H1_absflow", family="smearing_none",
   621	                  smearing="none")
   622	    evaluate_cell(panel, "HAR+ctrl", "H1_absflow", family="smearing_shared",
   623	                  smearing="shared")
   624	
   625	    gw = [r for r in REGISTRY if r.inference == "giacomini_white_qlike_fixed_window"]
   626	    assert len(gw) == 3
   627	    assert {r.family for r in gw} == {"primary", "smearing_none", "smearing_shared"}
   628	    assert all(r.feeds_gate for r in gw)
   629	
   630	
   631	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   632	# 8. Estimator integrity
   633	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   634	def test_normal_equations_match_lstsq(panel):
   635	    """The OOS loop solves the normal equations for speed (the power simulation
   636	    runs it ~16k times). It must be numerically identical to the SVD solution, or
   637	    the simulation and the headline would be using different estimators."""
   638	    d = panel.df.dropna(subset=SPECS["H1_absflow"])
   639	    X = np.column_stack([np.ones(len(d)), d[SPECS["H1_absflow"]].to_numpy(float)])
   640	    y = np.log(d["y"].to_numpy(float))
   641	    for start in (0, 100, 200):
   642	        xtr = X[start : start + GW_TRAIN_WINDOW]
   643	        ytr = y[start : start + GW_TRAIN_WINDOW]
   644	        np.testing.assert_allclose(
   645	            np.linalg.solve(xtr.T @ xtr, xtr.T @ ytr),
   646	            np.linalg.lstsq(xtr, ytr, rcond=None)[0],
   647	            rtol=1e-7,
   648	            atol=1e-9,
   649	        )
   650	
   651	
   652	def test_ols_helper_agrees_with_the_oos_loop(panel):
   653	    d = panel.df.dropna(subset=SPECS["HAR+ctrl"])
   654	    X = d[SPECS["HAR+ctrl"]].to_numpy(float)
   655	    y = np.log(d["y"].to_numpy(float))
   656	    Xc = np.column_stack([np.ones(len(X)), X])
   657	    np.testing.assert_allclose(
   658	        _ols(X, y), np.linalg.solve(Xc.T @ Xc, Xc.T @ y), rtol=1e-7, atol=1e-9
   659	    )
   660	
   661	
   662	def test_smearing_modes_produce_different_forecasts(panel):
   663	    """If none/own/shared all returned the same numbers, the smearing robustness
   664	    family would be theatre."""
   665	    own = paired_oos(panel, "HAR+ctrl", "H1_absflow", smearing="own")
   666	    none = paired_oos(panel, "HAR+ctrl", "H1_absflow", smearing="none")
   667	    shared = paired_oos(panel, "HAR+ctrl", "H1_absflow", smearing="shared")
   668	
   669	    assert not np.allclose(own.pred_aug, none.pred_aug)
   670	    assert not np.allclose(own.pred_aug, shared.pred_aug)
   671	    # 'shared' only overrides the AUGMENTED spec's multiplier
   672	    np.testing.assert_allclose(own.pred_base, shared.pred_base, rtol=1e-12)
   673	
   674	
   675	def test_forecasts_are_positive_variances(panel):
   676	    po = paired_oos(panel, "HAR+ctrl", "H1_absflow")
   677	    assert (po.pred_base > 0).all()
   678	    assert (po.pred_aug > 0).all()
   679	    assert (po.actual > 0).all()

282:- **ETH burn-in 敏感度**：主規格（INITIAL_TRAIN=250）只給 ETH 233 個樣本外原點，低於 preamble 的 252 日門檻。改用 200 → 樣本外 283/280 個原點（**達標**），DM t = 0.623 / −1.085，**判定不變**。
304:Codex 對抗性審查發現：yfinance 的日資料**缺了 2026-07-13**，且**包含當天還沒收完的 partial bar**。後果嚴重——`.shift(1)` 是按**列位置**位移，不是按**日曆日**；一旦日曆有洞，「t+1」目標會悄悄變成 **t+2**，而原本 `gap >= 1` 的不等式斷言**看不出來**。
313:### 6.2 Power test — 管線能不能找到真的存在的訊號？
315:`test_power_injected_signal_is_detected`：**植入**一條真正驅動次日 RV 的合成 flow（log RV_{t+1} = 持續性基底 + 1.2·|z_t|），要求管線把它找回來。
321:**但這只是「管線通了」，不是「設計有功效」**（獨立審查的正確批評）：這個植入訊號解釋了 70% 的變異、且 target 無雜訊。真正的功效問題由 §5.0 的 **MDE 曲線**在**真實面板**上回答——那才是決定本 null 能說什麼的東西。兩者缺一不可：power test 排除**程式錯誤**，MDE 曲線界定**宣稱範圍**。
323:### 6.3 Placebo test — 管線會不會無中生有？
325:`test_placebo_scrambled_flow_is_not_detected`：同一套機器餵入純雜訊 flow → 要求 **|DM t| < 3**。通過。
334:- **突變測試**：把 AR(5) 的 lag 向量反轉 → `test_unexpected_flow_ar5_lag_ordering` **確實失敗**（測試有牙齒，不是裝飾）。
337:- **測試套件**：**26 passed**（解析陷阱 / z-score 嚴格前向 / AR(5) lag 序 / 日曆對齊 / **h=5 重疊目標訓練過濾** / **方向閘門反轉** / power / placebo）。
339:> **結論：這個管線可證明地「找得到真訊號」（power test）、「不會無中生有」（placebo test）、且「評分方式不偏袒基準」（smearing test）。因此 NULL 是關於資料的陳述，不是關於程式的陳述。**
366:1. **ETH 面板 n=483 < 500**（原始 flow 504 obs，扣掉 20 日 z-score burn-in 與目標日）。ETH 的結論強度弱於 BTC。BTC n=621 達標。

### C1 — FIXED

你的理論主張站得住。GW 原文明確要求估計窗口有有限上界、排除 expanding window，並明說無條件 GW 統計量與 DM 統計量重合；不同的是 forecasting-method asymptotic experiment，估計不確定性不會消失，因此可處理 nested methods。[Giacomini–White 原文](https://onlinelibrary.wiley.com/doi/pdf/10.1111%2Fj.1468-0262.2006.00718.x)

rev1 確實不是只換函式名：共同 complete-case mask、共同 `start:end`、固定 250 筆、forward-label embargo 都真的落在 [k1709.py:858](/Users/yhlai0911/volpred-research/.claude/worktrees/dispatch-slot-2-c873d04d-k1709/experiments/k1709/k1709.py:858) 與 [k1709.py:909](/Users/yhlai0911/volpred-research/.claude/worktrees/dispatch-slot-2-c873d04d-k1709/experiments/k1709/k1709.py:909)。

但有兩個新瑕疵：

- fixed-window 那份「raw DM」不應再被描述成 `nested-biased`。它吃的就是同一條 GW-valid loss stream；只有 expanding diagnostic 才不具這個合法性。[k1709.py:1144](/Users/yhlai0911/volpred-research/.claude/worktrees/dispatch-slot-2-c873d04d-k1709/experiments/k1709/k1709.py:1144)
- 兩個統計量並非數值「完全相同」。GW lag covariance 除以 `n`，helper DM 除以 `n-lag`；例如 BTC H1 是 `1.298815` 對 `1.299319`。[results:866](/Users/yhlai0911/volpred-research/.claude/worktrees/dispatch-slot-2-c873d04d-k1709/experiments/k1709/k1709_results.json:866)

另有一個非 primary 問題：`unexpected_z` 的 AR(5) 實際用全部歷史資料擴張估計，卻被列入 fixed-memory full-family GW；不影響十個 primary cells，但「所有 GW robustness 都 bounded-memory」不成立。[k1709.py:537](/Users/yhlai0911/volpred-research/.claude/worktrees/dispatch-slot-2-c873d04d-k1709/experiments/k1709/k1709.py:537)

- **可以支撐的統計內容**：十個 primary fixed-window GW cells 沒有提供穩健的增量預測證據。
- **不能認證目前整份 rev1**：source 無法端到端重跑，exclusion/bounds 被 key collision 污染，README 又仍在宣稱已撤回的結果。
- 修正並完整重跑後，若 primary GW 數值不變，合理 verdict 只能是 **`INCONCLUSIVE_NO_EXACT_NULL_CLAIM`**；不能是 bounded null，更不能恢復任何 RV-space exclusion。

### C6 — PARTIAL

raw p 保留到 Holm、smearing runs 進 registry，原修法本身成立。

但新 registry 流程引入了阻斷性碰撞：

- `cell` 只含 asset/horizon/alt，不含 family、proxy、flow lag、threshold 或 train window。[k1709.py:1240](/Users/yhlai0911/volpred-research/.claude/worktrees/dispatch-slot-2-c873d04d-k1709/experiments/k1709/k1709.py:1240)
- `LOSS_CACHE[cell]` 與 `by_cell={...}` 都是 last-write-wins。[k1709.py:1867](/Users/yhlai0911/volpred-research/.claude/worktrees/dispatch-slot-2-c873d04d-k1709/experiments/k1709/k1709.py:1867)
- 因此 4/10 primary exclusion p 被 robustness cell 取代；例如 BTC h1 `.003054→.004806`、ETH h1 `.045727→.023967`。simultaneous bounds 也同樣被污染。
- `res["primary_cells"] = cells` 後仍繼續 append 同一 list，結果檔的 `primary_cells` 實際為 54 筆，與 `all_cells` 相同。[k1709.py:1732](/Users/yhlai0911/volpred-research/.claude/worktrees/dispatch-slot-2-c873d04d-k1709/experiments/k1709/k1709.py:1732)

目前碰撞碰巧沒有翻轉 `INCONCLUSIVE`，但 claim-bearing exclusion 數字與 confidence bounds 不可相信。

### 最終判決

目前沒有一致的 rev1 artifact：

- results JSON 是 `INCONCLUSIVE_NO_EXACT_NULL_CLAIM`。[results:12629](/Users/yhlai0911/volpred-research/.claude/worktrees/dispatch-slot-2-c873d04d-k1709/experiments/k1709/k1709_results.json:12629)
- README 仍是 v1，仍宣稱 `NULL` 與可排除 `+16.2% RV`。[README:3](/Users/yhlai0911/volpred-research/.claude/worktrees/dispatch-slot-2-c873d04d-k1709/experiments/k1709/README.md:3)
- renderer 對舊 results 會因 key drift 而 `KeyError`。[render_readme.py:348](/Users/yhlai0911/volpred-research/.claude/worktrees/dispatch-slot-2-c873d04d-k1709/experiments/k1709/render_readme.py:348)
- 現版單元測試是 52 passed，但沒有 main/render integration 或 cell-key uniqueness test。

所以：

- **可以支撐的統計內容**：十個 primary fixed-window GW cells 沒有提供穩健的增量預測證據。
- **不能認證目前整份 rev1**：source 無法端到端重跑，exclusion/bounds 被 key collision 污染，README 又仍在宣稱已撤回的結果。
- 修正並完整重跑後，若 primary GW 數值不變，合理 verdict 只能是 **`INCONCLUSIVE_NO_EXACT_NULL_CLAIM`**；不能是 bounded null，更不能恢復任何 RV-space exclusion。
